1081–1090: Role permission inspection
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from security.auth import get_current_user

ROLE_PERMISSIONS = {
    "admin": {
        "users:manage",
        "departments:manage",
        "disruptions:submit",
        "disruptions:read",
        "disruptions:read_public",
        "disruptions:review",
        "disruptions:assign",
        "analytics:read",
        "reports:read",
        "emergency:read",
        "emergency:manage",
        "resources:read",
        "resources:manage",
        "audit:read",
        "settings:manage",
    },

    "official": {
        "disruptions:read",
        "disruptions:review",
        "disruptions:assign",
        "analytics:read",
        "reports:read",
        "emergency:read",
        "emergency:manage",
        "resources:read",
        "resources:manage",
    },

    "analyst": {
        "disruptions:read",
        "analytics:read",
        "reports:read",
    },

    "emergency": {
        "disruptions:read",
        "emergency:read",
        "emergency:manage",
        "resources:read",
        "resources:manage",
    },

    "public": {
        "disruptions:submit",
        "disruptions:read_public",
    },
}


def require_roles(*allowed_roles):
    def dependency(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
        role = str(user.get("role", "public")).lower()

        if role not in allowed_roles:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Your role is not permitted to perform this action."
            )

        return user

    return dependency


def require_permission(permission):
    def dependency(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
        role = str(user.get("role", "public")).lower()

        if permission not in ROLE_PERMISSIONS.get(role, set()):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail=f"Permission denied: {permission}"
            )

        return user

    return dependency
